## Raw second transcription

```yaml
lines:
  - id: eic.step5_line1
    printed: "(Step 5, 1)"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 1z."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line1z}
  - id: eic.step5_line2
    printed: "(Step 5, 2)"
    label: "Enter the Medicaid waiver payment amounts excluded from income on Schedule 1, line 8s, unless you choose to include these amounts in earned income, in which case enter -0-."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    note: "No Schedule 1 line 8s input is in the closed interface; treated as zero."
  - id: eic.step5_line3
    printed: "(Step 5, 3)"
    label: "Subtract line 2 from line 1."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: eic.step5_line1, take: eic.step5_line2}
  - id: eic.step5_line4
    printed: "(Step 5, 4)"
    label: "Enter all of your nontaxable combat pay if you elect to include it in earned income."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    note: "No nontaxable combat pay amount input is in the closed interface; treated as zero."
  - id: eic.earned_income
    printed: "(Step 5, 5)"
    label: "Add lines 3 and 4. This is your earned income."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.step5_line3, eic.step5_line4]}
  - id: eic.wsb_4b
    printed: "4b"
    label: "Combine lines 1e, 2c, 3, and 4a. This is your total earned income."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.h_wsb_line1e, eic.h_wsb_line2c, eic.h_wsb_line3, eic.h_wsb_line4a]}
  - id: eic.wsb_6
    printed: "6 (eic.wsb_6)"
    label: "Enter your total earned income from Part 4, line 4b."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_4b_pos, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.wsb_4b
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_4b_pos, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.h_zero
  - id: eic.line1
    printed: "1"
    label: "Enter your earned income from Step 5."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: eic.earned_income}
  - id: eic.line2
    printed: "2"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 1 above in the EIC Table to find the credit. Enter the credit here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 3}]}
          then: eic.h_line1_c3
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 2}]}
          then: eic.h_line1_c2
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 1}]}
          then: eic.h_line1_c1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 0}]}
          then: eic.h_line1_c0
  - id: eic.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11b}
  - id: eic.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Are the amounts on lines 3 and 1 the same?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.line3, eic.line1]}
  - id: eic.line5
    printed: "5"
    label: "If the line 3 amount is below the threshold for the number of qualifying children with valid SSNs, leave line 5 blank; otherwise look up the amount on line 3 in the EIC Table and enter the credit here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line3_low_income, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line3_low_income, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.h_line5_table
  - id: eic.line6
    printed: "6 (eic.line6)"
    label: "This is your earned income credit."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.line2
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line3_low_income, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.line2
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line3_low_income, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.h_smaller_line5_line2
  - id: eic.self_employed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-employed, or filing Schedule SE as clergy/church employee, or filing Schedule C as a statutory employee."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [s1.has_sc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.clergy, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_statutory_any, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.credit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income credit."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_credit_b
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.h_credit_a
    note: "Step 2 tests not represented by the closed interface beyond investment income and Form 2555/excluded income are not separately screened here."
  - id: eic.check_27c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 27c."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_check_ssn, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_check_qc_other, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_check_us_home, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_check_line2_zero, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_check_wsb_4b, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_check_wsb5, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_check_wsb_line7, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.separated_box
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box in the Dependents section for the special rule for separated spouses."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfs_or_hoh, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.same_home, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.legally_separated, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}
    note: "Reading: true only for a non-joint MFS/HOH filer who lived apart or is legally separated and is not a qualifying child of another."
  - id: eic.clergy
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Filing Schedule SE because a member of the clergy or church employee income of $108.28 or more."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_clergy_count, {const: 0}]}
  - id: eic.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
  - id: eic.h_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Filing status is married filing jointly."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
  - id: eic.h_mfs_or_hoh
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Filing status is married filing separately or head of household."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "hoh"}]}
  - id: eic.h_eic_children
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying children for the EIC with valid SSNs."
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn_child, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_qc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying children for the EIC."
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_has_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "At least one qualifying child for the EIC."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0}]}
  - id: eic.h_no_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "No qualifying child for the EIC."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: false}]}
  - id: eic.h_statutory_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of Schedule C businesses filed as statutory employee."
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: sc
      field: sc.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [sc.statutory, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_statutory_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Any Schedule C business filed as statutory employee."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_statutory_count, {const: 0}]}
  - id: eic.h_clergy_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of persons with clergy or church employee income."
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [person.se_screen, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_income_under_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 1040 line 11b is below the Step 1 income limit."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, ge: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 3}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, {param: eic.limit3.joint}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, ge: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 3}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, {param: eic.limit3.other}]}
        - op: test
          all:

            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, {param: eic.limit2.joint}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 2}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, {param: eic.limit2.other}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 1}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, {param: eic.limit1.joint}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 1}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, {param: eic.limit1.other}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, {param: eic.limit0.joint}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, {param: eic.limit0.other}]}
  - id: eic.h_earned_under_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income is below the Step 5 question 3 limit."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, ge: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 3}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.earned_income, {param: eic.limit3.joint}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, ge: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 3}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.earned_income, {param: eic.limit3.other}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 2}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.earned_income, {param: eic.limit2.joint}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 2}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.earned_income, {param: eic.limit2.other}]}
        - op: test

            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.earned_income, {param: eic.limit1.joint}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 1}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.earned_income, {param: eic.limit1.other}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.earned_income, {param: eic.limit0.joint}]}
        - op: test
          all:

            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}

  - id: eic.h_line3_low_income
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet A line 3 is below the line 5 threshold."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, {param: eic.ws_threshold0.joint}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, {param: eic.ws_threshold0.other}]}
        - op: test

            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, {param: eic.ws_threshold1.joint}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, ge: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 1}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, {param: eic.ws_threshold1.other}]}
  - id: eic.h_line8_low_income
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 8 is below the line 10 threshold."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.wsb_line8, {param: eic.ws_threshold0.joint}]}
        - op: test

            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.wsb_line8, {param: eic.ws_threshold0.other}]}
        - op: test

            - {op: test, ge: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 1}]}

            - {op: test, lt: [eic.wsb_line8, {param: eic.ws_threshold1.other}]}
  - id: eic.h_age_eligible_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of persons age 25 through 64 at the end of 2025."
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [person.born_after_1960, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [person.born_before_2001, {const: true}]}
          - op: test
            any:
              - {op: test, eq: [person.role, {const: "taxpayer"}]}
              - op: test
                all:
                  - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
                  - {op: test, eq: [person.role, {const: "spouse"}]}
  - id: eic.h_age_eligible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "At least one taxpayer or spouse is age 25 through 64."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_age_eligible_count, {const: 0}]}
  - id: eic.h_step4_stop
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4 stop for a filer without a qualifying child."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_no_qc, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfs_or_hoh, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_age_eligible, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: false}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_check_ssn
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "No qualifying SSN for EIC."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [household.all_eic_ssn, {const: false}]}
  - id: eic.h_check_qc_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Could be a qualifying child of another person while not filing jointly."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_check_us_home
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "No qualifying child and main home not in the United States more than half of 2025."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_no_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: false}]}
  - id: eic.h_check_line2_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet A line 2 is zero."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.line2, eic.h_zero]}
  - id: eic.h_check_wsb_4b
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 4b is zero or less."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, le: [eic.wsb_4b, eic.h_zero]}
  - id: eic.h_check_wsb5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 4b is not below the Step 5 limit."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_under_limit, {const: false}]}
  - id: eic.h_check_wsb_line7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 7 is zero."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.wsb_line7, eic.h_zero]}
  - id: eic.h_line7a_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 1040 line 7a, not below zero."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: larger_of, of: [f1040.line7a, eic.h_zero]}
  - id: eic.h_investment_income
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Investment income for the EIC Step 2 test."
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: sum
      of:
        - f1040.line2a
        - f1040.line2b
        - f1040.line3b
        - eic.h_line7a_pos
  - id: eic.h_investment_over
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Investment income is more than $11,950."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_investment_income, {param: eic.investment_limit}]}
  - id: eic.h_investment_stop
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Investment income over the limit and no Form 4797 screening input."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_investment_over, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [sd.other_forms, {const: false}]}
  - id: eic.h_excluded_stop
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Excluded foreign income under Form 2555 or similar."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [sa.excluded_income, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_a_stop
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet A filer is stopped."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.check_27c, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_income_under_limit, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_earned_under_limit, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step4_stop, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_investment_stop, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_excluded_stop, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_b_stop
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B filer is stopped."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.check_27c, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_income_under_limit, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_under_limit, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_investment_stop, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_excluded_stop, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_credit_a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet A credit, gated by stops."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_a_stop, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_a_stop, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.line6
  - id: eic.h_credit_b
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B credit, gated by stops."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_b_stop, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_b_stop, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.wsb_line11
  - id: eic.h_line1_c0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup, line 1, no qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_0, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line1_c1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup, line 1, one qualifying child."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_1, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line1_c2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup, line 1, two qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_2, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line1_c3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup, line 1, three or more qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_3, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line3_c0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup, line 3, no qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_0, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line3_c1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup, line 3, one qualifying child."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_1, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line3_c2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup, line 3, two qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_2, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line3_c3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup, line 3, three or more qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_3, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line5_table
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet A line 5 EIC Table lookup."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 3}]}
          then: eic.h_line3_c3
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 2}]}
          then: eic.h_line3_c2
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 1}]}
          then: eic.h_line3_c1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 0}]}
          then: eic.h_line3_c0
  - id: eic.h_smaller_line5_line2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of Worksheet A lines 5 and 2."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [eic.line5, eic.line2]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line1a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 1a, Schedule SE Part I line 3."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: line3}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line1b
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 1b, Schedule SE Part I line 4b and line 5a."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: line4b}
    note: "The closed catalog does not provide Schedule SE line 5a; only line 4b is included."
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line1c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Combine Worksheet B lines 1a and 1b."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.h_wsb_line1a, eic.h_wsb_line1b]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line1d
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 1d, Schedule SE Part I line 13."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: line13}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line1e
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Subtract Worksheet B line 1d from line 1c."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: eic.h_wsb_line1c, take: eic.h_wsb_line1d}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line2a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 2a, net farm profit or loss."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    note: "No Schedule F or farm K-1 input is in the closed interface; treated as zero."
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line2b
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 2b, net profit or loss from Schedule C."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.line31}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line2c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Combine Worksheet B lines 2a and 2b."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.h_wsb_line2a, eic.h_wsb_line2b]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 3, Schedule C line 1 filed as statutory employee."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: sc
      field: sc.line1
      where: {op: test, eq: [sc.statutory, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line4a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 4a, earned income from Step 5."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: eic.earned_income}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_4b_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 4b is more than zero."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.wsb_4b, eic.h_zero]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb6_c0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup, Worksheet B line 6, no qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_0, key: eic.wsb_6, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_wsb6_c1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup, Worksheet B line 6, one qualifying child."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_1, key: eic.wsb_6, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_wsb6_c2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup, Worksheet B line 6, two qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_2, key: eic.wsb_6, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_wsb6_c3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup, Worksheet B line 6, three or more qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_3, key: eic.wsb_6, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.wsb_line7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 7, EIC Table lookup."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 3}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb6_c3
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 2}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb6_c2
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 1}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb6_c1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 0}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb6_c0
  - id: eic.wsb_line8
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 8, Form 1040 line 11b."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11b}
  - id: eic.wsb_line9
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Are Worksheet B lines 8 and 6 the same?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.wsb_line8, eic.wsb_6]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb10_c0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup, Worksheet B line 8, no qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_0, key: eic.wsb_line8, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_wsb10_c1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup, Worksheet B line 8, one qualifying child."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_1, key: eic.wsb_line8, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_wsb10_c2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup, Worksheet B line 8, two qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_2, key: eic.wsb_line8, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_wsb10_c3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup, Worksheet B line 8, three or more qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_3, key: eic.wsb_line8, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line10_table
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 10 EIC Table lookup."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 3}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb10_c3
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 2}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb10_c2
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 1}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb10_c1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eic_children, {const: 0}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb10_c0
  - id: eic.wsb_line10
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 10."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line8_low_income, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line8_low_income, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_line10_table
  - id: eic.h_smaller_line10_line7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of Worksheet B lines 10 and 7."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [eic.wsb_line10, eic.wsb_line7]}
  - id: eic.wsb_line11
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 11, earned income credit."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.wsb_line9, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.wsb_line7
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.wsb_line9, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line8_low_income, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.wsb_line7
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.wsb_line9, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line8_low_income, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.h_smaller_line10_line7
```
